# Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) 2026/2300 of 15 October 2026 granting a Union authorisation for the biocidal product family Active chlorine based products BPF – CID LINES NV in accordance with Regulation (EU) No 528/2012 of the European Parliament and of the Council

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Image omitted; see official source](https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/?uri=CELEX:32026R2300) | Official Journal of the European Union | EN L series |

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
|  | 2026/2300 | 28.10.2026 |

THE EUROPEAN COMMISSION,

Having regard to the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union,

Having regard to Regulation (EU) No 528/2012 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 22 May 2012 concerning the making available on the market and use of biocidal products [^ntr1-L_202602300EN.000101-E0001], and in particular Article 44(5), first paragraph, thereof,

Whereas:

- (1) On 19 December 2018, CID LINES NV submitted an application in accordance with Article 43(1) of Regulation (EU) No 528/2012 for authorisation of a biocidal product family named ‘Active chlorine based products BPF – CID LINES NV’ of product-types 2, 3, 4 and 5, as described in Annex V to that Regulation, providing written confirmation that the competent authority of Belgium had agreed to evaluate the application. The application was recorded under case number BC-MY047028-07 in the Register for Biocidal Products.

- (2) ‘Active chlorine based products BPF – CID LINES NV’ contains active chlorine released from sodium hypochlorite as the active substance, which is included in the Union list of approved active substances referred to in Article 9(2) of Regulation (EU) No 528/2012 for product-types 2, 3, 4 and 5.

- (3) On 23 March 2021, the evaluating competent authority submitted, in accordance with Article 44(1) of Regulation (EU) No 528/2012, an assessment report and the conclusions of its evaluation to the European Chemicals Agency (‘the Agency’).

- (4) On 4 November 2021, the Agency submitted to the Commission an opinion [^ntr2-L_202602300EN.000101-E0002], including the draft summary of the biocidal product characteristics (‘SPC’) of ‘Active chlorine-based products BPF – CID LINES NV’ and the final assessment report on the biocidal product family in accordance with Article 44(3) of Regulation (EU) No 528/2012.

- (5) The opinion concludes that ‘Active chlorine based products BPF – CID LINES NV’ is a biocidal product family within the meaning of Article 3(1), point (s), of Regulation (EU) No 528/2012, that it is eligible for Union authorisation in accordance with Article 42(1) of that Regulation and that subject to compliance with the draft SPC, it meets the conditions laid down in Article 19(6) of that Regulation.

- (6) On 22 November 2021, the Agency transmitted to the Commission the draft SPC in all the official languages of the Union in accordance with Article 44(4) of Regulation (EU) No 528/2012.

- (7) On 24 June 2022, the Standing Committee on Biocidal products questioned the risk mitigation measures proposed in the Agency opinion to protect operators against irreversible damages of applying corrosive particles by coarse pressure spraying. The Commission considered appropriate to postpone its decision until appropriate measures were identified to mitigate that risk.

- (8) Following further discussions with the Agency and the evaluating competent authority, on 28 April 2023, the Commission requested the Agency in accordance with Article 75(1), point (g), of Regulation (EU) No 528/2012 to clarify whether the wearing of personal protective equipment or respiratory protective equipment for the application of biocidal products classified for skin and eyes corrosion Category 1A and corrosive to the respiratory tract in accordance with Regulation (EC) No 1272/2008 [^ntr3-L_202602300EN.000101-E0003] and applied by foaming and coarse pressure spraying is in line with the Agency technical guidance [^ntr4-L_202602300EN.000101-E0004], and if so, to clarify for which working conditions this could apply and if any additional risk mitigations measures would be required.

- (9) On 11 September 2025, the Agency issued an opinion [^ntr5-L_202602300EN.000101-E0005], in which it concluded that the use of protective equipment for the application of biocidal products classified for skin and eyes corrosion Category 1A and corrosive to the respiratory tract and applied by foaming and coarse pressure spraying can be acceptable and is in line with the Agency technical guidance.

- (10) The Commission concurs with the opinions of the Agency and considers it therefore appropriate to grant a Union authorisation for ‘Active chlorine based products BPF – CID LINES NV’.

- (11) In its opinion of 11 September 2025, the Agency recommends that the authorisation holder conducts a self-accelerating decomposition temperature (SADT) test for ‘Active chlorine-based products BPF – CID LINES NV’ as a condition in the authorisation. That test should confirm that the products in meta-SPCs 1, 3 to 11 and 13 to 15 are not to be classified as self-reactive mixture.

- (12) The Commission agrees with that recommendation and considers that the submission of the results of that test confirming the non-classification should be a condition relating to the making available on the market and use of the biocidal products in ‘Active chlorine based products BPF – CID LINES NV’ in meta SPCs 1, 3 to 11 and 13 to 15 pursuant to Article 22(1) of Regulation (EU) No 528/2012. The Commission also considers that the fact that data is to be provided after the authorisation is granted does not affect the conclusion on the fulfilment of the condition under Article 19(6) of that Regulation based on existing data.

- (13) The measures provided for in this Regulation are in accordance with the opinion of the Standing Committee on biocidal products,

HAS ADOPTED THIS REGULATION:

#### Article 1

A Union authorisation with authorisation number EU-0027465-0000 is granted to CID LINES NV for the making available on the market and use of the biocidal product family ‘Active chlorine based products BPF – CID LINES NV’ subject to compliance with the terms and conditions set out in Annex I and in accordance with the summary of the biocidal product characteristics set out in Annex II.

The Union Authorisation is valid from 17 November 2026 until 31 October 2036.

#### Article 2

This Regulation shall enter into force on the twentieth day following that of its publication in the *Official Journal of the European Union*.

This Regulation shall be binding in its entirety and directly applicable in all Member States.

Done at Brussels, 15 October 2026.

***For the Commission***

***The President***

**Ursula VON DER LEYEN**

## ANNEX I

## TERMS AND CONDITIONS (EU-0027465-0000)

The authorisation holder shall conduct a self-accelerating decomposition temperature (SADT) test for ‘Active chlorine-based products BPF – CID LINES NV’ to confirm that the biocidal products in meta SPCs 1, 3 to 11 and 13 to 15 are not to be classified as a self-reactive mixture.

By 17 November 2027 the authorisation holder shall submit the results of the test confirming the non-classification to the Agency.

## ANNEX II

## SUMMARY OF PRODUCT CHARACTERISTICS FOR A BIOCIDAL PRODUCT FAMILY

Active chlorine based products BPF – CID LINES NV

**Product type(s)**

PT02: Disinfectants and algaecides not intended for direct application to humans or animals

PT04: Food and feed area

PT03: Veterinary hygiene

PT05: Drinking water

**Authorisation number:** EU-0027465-0000

**R4BP asset number:** U-0027465-0000

### 4. AUTHORISED USE(S) OF THE META SPC

#### 4.1   **Use description**

### **PT2 - Use # 1.1: Disinfection of hard non-porous surfaces and equipment by brushing, foaming, spraying, pouring followed by wiping – professional use**

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Product type | PT02: Disinfectants and algaecides not intended for direct application to humans or animals |

| Field(s) of use | indoor use In Households, industry, institutions (such as offices or prison), public areas and hotels (not in healthcare areas): Disinfection of hard/non-porous surfaces, without prior cleaning |
| Application method(s) | Method: Surface disinfection by brushing, foaming, spraying, pouring followed by wiping Detailed description: Concentrated product, to be diluted. For disinfection procedures by :- Brushing, - Foaming: low pressure foaming device or trigger foam, - Spraying: low pressure coarse spraying device or trigger spray, - Pouring followed by wiping. Rinse after disinfection with water of drinking water quality. |

| Category(ies) of users | professional |
| Pack sizes and packaging material | — Bottle: 1 L, 1 Kg — Drum: 5 L, 10 L, 20 L, 25 L, 30 L, 60 L, 200 L, 220 L, 5 kg, 10 kg, 12 kg, 20 kg, 25 kg, 30 kg, 60 kg, 200 kg, 220 kg, 240 kg — Container: 600 L, 1 000 L, 1 100 L, 600 kg, 1 000  kg, 1 100  kg HDPE (High Density PolyEthylene) |

##### 4.1.1   *Use-specific instructions for use*

Mixing and loading:

- — Manual mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and product is poured in the recipient containing the relevant volume of water to obtain the necessary dilution.

- — Semi-automated mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and hose is placed in the can. Then transfer the product by manual pumping or with a semi-automatic pump.

- — Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Dilution:

Bacteria, yeasts, fungi, virus: Dilute the product at 25 %. (Dose 25 ml product to prepare a 100 mL solution)

Application:

Application can be done by brushing (apply by using a brush), foaming (apply by using a low pressure foaming device or trigger foam), spraying (apply by using a low pressure coarse spraying device or trigger spray), pouring followed by wiping (apply by using a wipe).

Application rate is 250 mL/m<sup>2</sup>. Foaming and spraying are done at low pressure avoiding the formation of aerosols. Make sure to foam/spray/ brush/pour and wipe sufficient liquid to keep the surface wet for at least 25 minutes.

Brushing: a brush with a handle has to be used to apply the diluted solution.

Pour the diluted solution directly on the surface and brush.

The application rate is a worst-case value. Apply the minimum quantity of product in order to cover the entire surface and in order for the surface to remain wet until the end of contact time.

Rinse the surfaces after use with water of drinking water quality.

Post-application: empty containers are disposed of.

Frequency: to be used in between production and/or part of daily cleaning.

##### 4.1.2   *Use-specific risk mitigation measures*

See general directions for use.

Do not immerse hands in the solution.

Do not (use/apply) directly on or near food, feed or drinks, or on surfaces or utensils likely to be in direct contact with food, feed, drinks and livestock.

Do not apply directly to surfaces on which food or feed is stored, prepared or eaten.

##### 4.1.3   *Where specific to the use, the particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment*

See general directions for use.

##### 4.1.4   *Where specific to the use, the instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging*

See general directions for use.

##### 4.1.5   *Where specific to the use, the conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage*

See general directions for use.

### 5. GENERAL DIRECTIONS FOR USE OF THE META SPC 1

#### 5.1   **Instructions for use**

See use-specific instructions for each use.

Do not mix with other chemicals.

Disinfection cycle :

- — Products must be diluted in clean water before use.

- — Dilution rate & contact time depends on the use considered. Please refer to the description of application method related to each use.

- — Final rinsing (with water of drinking water quality) is required.

Surface disinfection procedures **by spraying, foaming, brushing or pouring followed by wiping** : The surfaces to be disinfected must be wet enough in order to keep them wet during the preconised contact time for optimal disinfection.

The following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘Make sure to wet surfaces completely’.

#### 5.2   **Risk mitigation measures**

During mixing and loading, application (brushing/pouring and wiping) and post application step:

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves during product handling phase in line with EN 374 or equivalent (glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

The use of eye protection in line with EN 166 or equivalent during handling of the product is mandatory.

A protective coverall (at least type 3, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

During application (trigger spraying/foaming):

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves during product handling phase in line with EN 374 or equivalent (glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

The use of eye protection in line with EN 166 or equivalent during handling of the product is mandatory.

A protective coverall (at least type 4, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

Use of respiratory protective equipment (RPE) in line with EN 136 or equivalent providing a protection factor of 10 is mandatory. At least a powered air purifying respirator with helmet/hood/mask (TH1/TM1), or a half/full mask with particle filter P2 is required.

During application by foaming and by coarse spraying

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves during product handling phase in line with EN 374 or equivalent.

A protective coverall, with hood (at least type 4, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wearing oversleeves for wrists and ankles is mandatory.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

Using a face shield/face mask is mandatory.

Respiratory protective equipment (RPE) providing a protection factor of 40 is mandatory. At least a powered air purifying respirator with helmet/hood/mask (TH3/TM3), or a full-face mask (EN 136 or equivalent) with particle filter (EN 143 filters or equivalent), type P3.

The use of these protective equipment is without prejudice to the application of Council Directive 98/24/EC and other Union legislation in the area of health and safety at work. See section 6 for the full references to this act and the European Standards.

Apply the product with a lance to increase the distance between the user and the source of emission.

After disinfection, treated surfaces should be thoroughly rinsed with water of drinking water quality.

The same PPEs/RPE have to be worn during the rinsing of the room.

Necessary specifications for safe and adequate use of PPE during the application of corrosive product have to be specified by the authorisation holder.

Training must be ensured for the users to ensure appropriate handling of the product and the PPE required.

To limit exposure during the removal of PPEs, PPEs have to be cleaned after each use and before removal.

Trigger spraying/foaming:

In order to reduce inhalatory exposure product may only be applied by trigger spraying device delivering coarse droplets (MMAD > 100). Please refer to device supplier for technical details.

Foaming and Coarse spraying: In order to reduce inhalatory exposure product may only be applied by coarse spraying. Please refer to device supplier for technical details.

Ensure that the application by foaming or by coarse spraying is carried out with an adequate ventilation. The ventilation needs to be specified by the Authorisation holder within the product information.

After disinfection by foaming or by coarse spraying, the treated area can be re-entered only when an ambient air concentration of chlorine is ensured to be below 0,5 mg/m<sup>3</sup>.

If no appropriate method to determine the chlorine air concentration after use is available, contact the supplier.

Wash hands after use.

Apply in the absence of humans and animals and out of their reach.

No access of the general public during treatment and until surfaces are dried.

Keep away from pets/keep out of reach of pets.

#### 5.3   **Particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment**

IF INHALED: Move to fresh air and keep at rest in a position comfortable for breathing.

If symptoms: Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

If no symptoms: Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF SWALLOWED: Immediately rinse mouth. Give something to drink, if exposed person is able to swallow. Do NOT induce vomiting. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

IF ON SKIN: Immediately wash skin with plenty of water. Thereafter take off all contaminated clothing and wash it before reuse. Continue to wash the skin with water for 15 minutes. Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF IN EYES: Immediately rinse with water for several minutes. Remove contact lenses, if present and easy to do. Continue rinsing for at least 15 minutes. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

Information to Healthcare personnel/doctor:

The eyes should also be rinsed repeatedly on the way to the doctor if eye exposure to alkaline chemicals (pH > 11), amines and acids like acetic acid, formic acid or propionic acid

#### 5.4   **Instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging**

At the end of the treatment, dispose unused product and the packaging in accordance with local requirements. Used product can be flushed to municipal sewer or disposed to the manure deposit depending on local requirements. Avoid release to an individual waste water treatment plant.

#### 5.5   **Conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage**

Do not store above 30 °C and store protected from sunlight. Keep in original container.

Store in dry, cool, well-ventilated area. Provide local exhaust or general room ventilation to minimize mist and/or vapour concentrations. Keep container closed when not in use. Minimize exposure to air and light.

The shelf-life of the product is 12 months. After opening the cans, products are stable during 6 months.

Sodium chlorate may be formed during storage. To avoid increasing formation of this degradation product, do not exceed the established shelf life of the biocidal product.

### 6 OTHER INFORMATION

In the eventuality of abstraction of drinking water from surface water/groundwater, the chlorate drinking water limit of 700 μg/L should be monitored.

‘Professionals (including industrial users)’ means trained professional if this is required by national legislation.

The full titles of the EN standards referenced in sections ‘general risk mitigation measures’ are :

EN ISO 374-1:2016 Protective gloves against dangerous chemicals and micro-organisms – Part 1: Terminology and performance requirements for chemical risks (ISO 374-1:2016)

EN 166:2001 Personal eye-protection – Specifications

EN 14605:2005+A1:2009 Protective clothing against liquid chemicals – performance requirements for clothing with liquid-tight (Type 3) or spray-tight (Type 4) connections, including items providing protection to parts of the body only (Types PB [3] and PB [4])

EN 13832-3:2018 Footwear protecting against chemicals – Part 3: Requirements for prolonged contact with chemicals

EN 136:1998 Respiratory protective devices – Full face masks – Requirements, testing, marking EN 136:1998/AC:2003

EN 143:2021 Respiratory protective devices – Particle filters – Requirements, testing, marking

Council Directive 98/24/EC of 7 April 1998 on the protection of the health and safety of workers from the risks related to chemical agents at work (fourteenth individual Directive within the meaning of Article 16(1) of Directive 89/391/EEC) ([OJ L 131, 5.5.1998, p. 11](http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_1998_131_R_TOC))

Dilution:

Bacteria, yeasts, fungi, virus: The product is used pure.

Application:

Brushing: a brush with a handle has to be used to apply the solution.

Pour the solution directly on the surface and brush.

Rinse the surfaces after use with water of drinking water quality.

Post-application: empty containers are disposed of.

### 5 GENERAL DIRECTIONS FOR USE OF THE META SPC 2

#### 5.1   **Instructions for use**

See use-specific instructions for each use

Do not mix with other chemicals.

Disinfection cycle :

- — Contact time depends on the use considered. Please refer to the description of application method related to each use.

- — Final rinsing (with water of drinking water quality) is required.

Surface disinfection procedures **by spraying, foaming, brushing or pouring followed by wiping** : The surfaces to be disinfected must be wet enough in order to keep them wet during the preconised contact time for optimal disinfection.

The following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘Make sure to wet surfaces completely’.

#### 5.2   **Risk mitigation measures**

During loading, application (brushing, pouring followed by wiping) and post application step:

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves during product handling phase in line with EN 374 or equivalent (glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

The use of eye protection

in line with EN 166 or equivalent

during handling of the product is mandatory.

A protective coverall (at least type 3, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

During trigger spraying/foaming:

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves during product handling phase

in line with EN 374 or equivalent

(glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

The use of eye protection

in line with EN 166 or equivalent

during handling of the product is mandatory.

A protective coverall (at least type 4, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

The use of these protective equipment is without prejudice to the application of Council Directive 98/24/EC and other Union legislation in the area of health and safety at work. See section 6 for the full references to this act and the European Standards.

Ensure adequate ventilation during the application.

After disinfection, treated surfaces should be thoroughly rinsed with water of drinking water quality.

No access of the general public during treatment and until surfaces are dried.

Keep away from pets/keep out of reach of pets.

#### 5.3   **Particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment**

IF INHALED: Move to fresh air and keep at rest in a position comfortable for breathing.

If symptoms: Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

If no symptoms: Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF SWALLOWED: Immediately rinse mouth. Give something to drink, if exposed person is able to swallow. Do NOT induce vomiting. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

IF ON SKIN: Immediately wash skin with plenty of water. Thereafter take off all contaminated clothing and wash it before reuse. Continue to wash the skin with water for 15 minutes. Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF IN EYES: Immediately rinse with water for several minutes. Remove contact lenses, if present and easy to do. Continue rinsing for at least 15 minutes. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

Information to Healthcare personnel/doctor:

The eyes should also be rinsed repeatedly on the way to the doctor if eye exposure to alkaline chemicals (pH > 11), amines and acids like acetic acid, formic acid or propionic acid

#### 5.4   **Instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging**

At the end of the treatment, dispose unused product and the packaging in accordance with local requirements. Used product can be flushed to municipal sewer or disposed to the manure deposit depending on local requirements. Avoid release to an individual waste water treatment plant.

#### 5.5   **Conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage**

Do not store above 30 °C and store protected from sunlight. Keep in original container.

Store in dry, cool, well-ventilated area. Provide local exhaust or general room ventilation to minimize mist and/or vapour concentrations. Keep container closed when not in use. Minimize exposure to air and light.

The shelf-life if the product is 12 months. After opening the cans, products are stable during 6 months.

Sodium chlorate may be formed during storage. To avoid increasing formation of this degradation product, do not exceed the established shelf life of the biocidal product.

### 6 OTHER INFORMATION

In the eventuality of abstraction of drinking water from surface water/groundwater, the chlorate drinking water limit of 700 μg/L should be monitored.

‘Professionals (including industrial users)’ means trained professional if this is required by national legislation.

The full titles of the EN standards referenced in sections ‘general risk mitigation measures’ are :

Mixing and loading:

Professionals:

Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Loading: by pouring the product in the toilets

Dilution:

bacteria, fungi/yeasts and viruses: The product is used pure.

Application:

### 5 GENERAL DIRECTIONS FOR USE OF THE META SPC 3

#### 5.1   **Instructions for use**

Comply with the instructions for use.

See use-specific instructions for each use

Do not mix with other chemicals.

Disinfection procedures **by pouring** :

The surfaces to be disinfected must be wet enough in order to keep them wet during the preconised contact time for optimal disinfection.

The following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘Make sure to wet surfaces completely’.

#### 5.2   **Risk mitigation measures**

Professionals - Fully automated mixing and loading:

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves during product handling phase in line with EN 374 or equivalent (glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

The use of eye protection in line with EN 166 or equivalent during handling of the product is mandatory.

A protective coverall (at least type 3, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

The use of these protective equipment is without prejudice to the application of Council Directive 98/24/EC and other Union legislation in the area of health and safety at work. See section 6 for the full references to this act and the European Standards.

Professionals and Non-professionals - application:

Wash hands thoroughly after handling.

Wash face/eyes after accidental exposure.

Keep away from children.

Apply only into the rim of the toilet to avoid skin contact.

Avoid splashing.

Avoid contact with skin and eyes.

Do not brush the product in toilet bowl.

Flush toilet after contact time.

No access of the general public during treatment and until surfaces are dried.

Keep away from pets/keep out of reach of pets.

#### 5.3   **Particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment**

If medical advice is needed, have product container or label at hand.

IF INHALED: Move to fresh air and keep at rest in a position comfortable for breathing.

If symptoms: Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

If no symptoms: Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF SWALLOWED: Immediately rinse mouth. Give something to drink, if exposed person is able to swallow. Do NOT induce vomiting. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

IF ON SKIN: Immediately wash skin with plenty of water. Thereafter take off all contaminated clothing and wash it before reuse. Continue to wash the skin with water for 15 minutes. Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF IN EYES: Immediately rinse with water for several minutes. Remove contact lenses, if present and easy to do. Continue rinsing for at least 15 minutes. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

Information to Healthcare personnel/doctor:

The eyes should also be rinsed repeatedly on the way to the doctor if eye exposure to alkaline chemicals (pH > 11), amines and acids like acetic acid, formic acid or propionic acid

#### 5.4   **Instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging**

At the end of the treatment, dispose unused product and the packaging in accordance with local requirements. Used product can be flushed to municipal sewer or disposed to the manure deposit depending on local requirements. Avoid release to an individual waste water treatment plant.

#### 5.5   **Conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage**

Keep out of reach of children and non-target animals/pets.

Do not store above 30 °C and store protected from sunlight. Keep in original container.

Store in dry, cool, well-ventilated area. Provide local exhaust or general room ventilation to minimize mist and/or vapour concentrations. Keep container closed when not in use. Minimize exposure to air and light.

The shelf-life if the product is 12 months. After opening the cans, products are stable during 6 months.

Sodium chlorate may be formed during storage. To avoid increasing formation of this degradation product, do not exceed the established shelf life of the biocidal product.

##### 4.1.1   *Use-specific instructions for use*

Mixing and loading:

Pipelines and tanks are empty before disinfection.

Manual mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and product is poured in the recipient containing the relevant volume of water to obtain the necessary dilution.

Semi-automated mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and hose is placed in the can. Then transfer the product by manual pumping or with a semi-automatic pump.

Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Dilution:

See application rates & frequency section

Application:

In clean conditions: Prior to disinfection, clean the surfaces

Application in CIP with circulation: Add the product and water into the system to obtain the required concentration of use. Circulate for the required contact time at the required temperature.

Application in CIP without circulation: Add the product and water into the system to obtain the required concentration of use. Use enough working solution to cover all surfaces. Inner surfaces should remain wet during the required contact time at the required temperature.

Post-application: The pipeline or tank is rinsed with water of drinking water quality under closed system. Empty containers are disposed of.

Frequency: to be used in between production cycles as intermediate cleaning and/or part of daily cleaning

##### 4.1.2   *Use-specific risk mitigation measures*

See general directions for use.

Manual mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and product is poured in the recipient containing the relevant volume of water to obtain the necessary dilution.

Semi-automated mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and hose is placed in the can. Then transfer the product by manual pumping or with a semi-automatic pump.

Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Dilution:

See application rates & frequency section

Application:

In clean conditions: Prior to disinfection, clean the surfaces

Application in CIP with circulation: Add the product and water into the system to obtain the required concentration of use. Circulate for the required contact time at the required temperature.

Application in CIP without circulation (including soaking): Add the product and water into the system to obtain the required concentration of use. Use enough working solution to cover all surfaces. Inner surfaces should remain wet during the required contact time at the required temperature.

Post-application: The pipeline or tank is rinsed with water of drinking water quality under closed system. Empty containers are disposed of.

Frequency: To be used in between food production as intermediate cleaning and/or part of daily cleaning

##### 4.2.2   *Use-specific risk mitigation measures*

See general directions for use.

For food commodities, ensure that the concentration of chlorate present in food does not exceed the MRL values set in Regulation 2020/749.

### 5 GENERAL DIRECTIONS FOR USE OF THE META SPC 4

#### 5.1   **Instructions for use**

See use-specific instructions for each use

Do not mix with other chemicals.

The surfaces to be disinfected must be cleaned before the disinfection procedure if required.

For the relevant uses, the following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label: ‘Thoroughly clean, rinse and remove excess water on the surfaces to be disinfected’.

Disinfection cycle :

- — Products must be diluted in clean water before use.

- — Dilution rate & contact time depends on the use considered. Please refer to the description of application method related to each use.

- — Final rinsing (with water of drinking water quality) is required.

Surface disinfection procedures **by soaking/dipping/CIP/closed systems** : According to the Applicant, the soaking/dipping/CIP solution is prepared *ad hoc* (never in advance) for a single-time use. The bath/CIP solution is not intended to be re-used.

The following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘The soaking/dipping bath/CIP solution should be prepared *ad hoc* (never in advance) and used only once’.

#### 5.2   **Risk mitigation measures**

During mixing and loading step, maintenance and repair:

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves during product handling phase in line with EN 374 or equivalent (glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

The use of eye protection in line with EN 166 or equivalent during handling of the product is mandatory.

A protective coverall (at least type 3, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

Maintenance and repair, pressurized systems:

Use of respiratory protective equipment (RPE) in line with EN 136 or equivalent providing a protection factor of 10 is mandatory. At least a powered air purifying respirator with helmet/hood/mask (TH1/TM1), or a half/full mask with particle filter P2 is required.

The use of these protective equipment is without prejudice to the application of Council Directive 98/24/EC and other Union legislation in the area of health and safety at work. See section 6 for the full references to this act and the European Standards.

Ensure adequate ventilation during the application.

After disinfection, treated surfaces should be thoroughly rinsed with water of drinking water quality.

#### 5.3   **Particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment**

IF INHALED: Move to fresh air and keep at rest in a position comfortable for breathing.

If symptoms: Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

If no symptoms: Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF SWALLOWED: Immediately rinse mouth. Give something to drink, if exposed person is able to swallow. Do NOT induce vomiting. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

IF ON SKIN: Immediately wash skin with plenty of water. Thereafter take off all contaminated clothing and wash it before reuse. Continue to wash the skin with water for 15 minutes. Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF IN EYES: Immediately rinse with water for several minutes. Remove contact lenses, if present and easy to do. Continue rinsing for at least 15 minutes. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

Information to Healthcare personnel/doctor:

The eyes should also be rinsed repeatedly on the way to the doctor if eye exposure to alkaline chemicals (pH > 11), amines and acids like acetic acid, formic acid or propionic acid

#### 5.4   **Instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging**

At the end of the treatment, dispose unused product and the packaging in accordance with local requirements. Used product can be flushed to municipal sewer or disposed to the manure deposit depending on local requirements. Avoid release to an individual waste water treatment plant.

#### 5.5   **Conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage**

Do not store above 30 °C and store protected from sunlight. Keep in original container.

Store in dry, cool, well-ventilated area. Provide local exhaust or general room ventilation to minimize mist and/or vapour concentrations. Keep container closed when not in use. Minimize exposure to air and light.

The shelf-life of the product is 9 months. After opening the cans, products are stable during 6 months.

Sodium chlorate may be formed during storage. To avoid increasing formation of this degradation product, do not exceed the established shelf life of the biocidal product.

### 6 OTHER INFORMATION

In the eventuality of abstraction of drinking water from surface water/groundwater, the chlorate drinking water limit of 700 μg/L should be monitored.

‘Professionals (including industrial users)’ means trained professional if this is required by national legislation.

The full titles of the EN standards referenced in sections ‘general risk mitigation measures’ are :

EN ISO 374-1:2016 Protective gloves against dangerous chemicals and micro-organisms – Part 1: Terminology and performance requirements for chemical risks (ISO 374-1:2016)

EN 166:2001 Personal eye-protection – Specifications

EN 14605:2005+A1:2009 Protective clothing against liquid chemicals – performance requirements for clothing with liquid-tight (Type 3) or spray-tight (Type 4) connections, including items providing protection to parts of the body only (Types PB [3] and PB [4])

EN 13832-3:2018 Footwear protecting against chemicals – Part 3: Requirements for prolonged contact with chemicals

EN 136:1998 Respiratory protective devices – Full face masks – Requirements, testing, marking EN 136:1998/AC:2003

EN 143:2021 Respiratory protective devices – Particle filters – Requirements, testing, marking

Council Directive 98/24/EC of 7 April 1998 on the protection of the health and safety of workers from the risks related to chemical agents at work (fourteenth individual Directive within the meaning of Article 16(1) of Directive 89/391/EEC) ([OJ L 131, 5.5.1998, p. 11](http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_1998_131_R_TOC))

### 3 HAZARD AND PRECAUTIONARY STATEMENTS OF THE META SPC 5

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Hazard statements | H290: May be corrosive to metals. H314: Causes severe skin burns and eye damage. H410: Very toxic to aquatic life with long lasting effects. |
| Precautionary statements | P260: Do not breathe vapours. P260: Do not breathe spray. P260: Do not breathe mist. P273: Avoid release to the environment. P280: Wear protective gloves/protective clothing/eye protection/face protection. P301+P330+P331: IF SWALLOWED: rinse mouth. Do NOT induce vomiting. P303+P361+P353: IF ON SKIN (or hair): Take off immediately all contaminated clothing. Rinse skin with water [or shower]. P304+P340: IF INHALED: Remove person to fresh air and keep comfortable for breathing. P305+P351+P338: IF IN EYES: Rinse cautiously with water for several minutes. Remove contact lenses, if present and easy to do. Continue rinsing. P310: Immediately call a POISON CENTER/doctor. P501: Dispose of contents hazardous or special waste collection point, in accordance with local, regional, national and/or international regulation. P501: Dispose of container hazardous or special waste collection point, in accordance with local, regional, national and/or international regulation. P391: Collect spillage. |

##### 4.1.1   *Use-specific instructions for use*

Mixing and loading:

Pipelines and tanks are empty before disinfection.

Manual mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and product is poured in the recipient containing the relevant volume of water to obtain the necessary dilution.

Semi-automated mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and hose is placed in the can. Then transfer the product by manual pumping or with a semi-automatic pump.

Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Dilution:

See application rates & frequency section.

Application:

In clean conditions: Prior to disinfection, clean the surfaces.

Application in CIP with circulation: Add the product and water into the system to obtain the required concentration of use. Circulate for the required contact time at the required temperature.

Application in CIP without circulation: Add the product and water into the system to obtain the required concentration of use. Use enough working solution to cover all surfaces. Inner surfaces should remain wet during the required contact time at the required temperature.

Post-application: The pipeline or tank is rinsed with water of drinking water quality under closed system. Empty containers are disposed of.

Frequency: To be used in between production cycles as intermediate cleaning and/or part of daily cleaning.

##### 4.1.2   *Use-specific risk mitigation measures*

See general directions for use.

### 5 GENERAL DIRECTIONS FOR USE OF THE META SPC 5

#### 5.1   **Instructions for use**

See use-specific instructions for each use

Do not mix with other chemicals.

- 1) The surfaces to be disinfected must be cleaned before the disinfection procedure if required.

For the relevant uses, the following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label: ‘thoroughly clean, rinse and remove excess water on the surfaces to be disinfected’.

- 2) Disinfection cycle :

- — Products must be diluted in clean water before use.

- — Dilution rate & contact time depends on the use considered. Please refer to the description of application method related to each use.

- — Final rinsing (with water of drinking water quality) is required.

Surface disinfection procedures **by soaking/dipping/CIP/closed systems** : According to the Applicant, the soaking/dipping/CIP solution is prepared *ad hoc* (never in advance) for a single-time use. The bath/CIP solution is not intended to be re-used.

The following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘The soaking/dipping bath/CIP solution should be prepared *ad hoc* (never in advance) and used only once’.

#### 5.2   **Risk mitigation measures**

During mixing and loading step, maintenance and repair:

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves in line with EN 374 or equivalent during product handling phase (glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

The use of eye protection in line with EN 166 or equivalent during handling of the product is mandatory.

A protective coverall (at least type 3, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

Maintenance and repair, pressurized systems:

Use of respiratory protective equipment (RPE) in line with EN 136 or equivalent providing a protection factor of 10 is mandatory. At least a powered air purifying respirator with helmet/hood/mask (TH1/TM1), or a half/full mask with particle filter P2 is required.

The use of these protective equipment is without prejudice to the application of Council Directive 98/24/EC and other Union legislation in the area of health and safety at work. See section 6 for the full references to this act and the European Standards.

Ensure adequate ventilation during the application.

After disinfection, treated surfaces should be thoroughly rinsed with water of drinking water quality.

#### 5.3   **Particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment**

IF INHALED: Move to fresh air and keep at rest in a position comfortable for breathing.

If symptoms: Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

If no symptoms: Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF SWALLOWED: Immediately rinse mouth. Give something to drink, if exposed person is able to swallow. Do NOT induce vomiting. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

IF ON SKIN: Immediately wash skin with plenty of water. Thereafter take off all contaminated clothing and wash it before reuse. Continue to wash the skin with water for 15 minutes. Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF IN EYES: Immediately rinse with water for several minutes. Remove contact lenses, if present and easy to do. Continue rinsing for at least 15 minutes. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

Information to Healthcare personnel/doctor:

The eyes should also be rinsed repeatedly on the way to the doctor if eye exposure to alkaline chemicals (pH > 11), amines and acids like acetic acid, formic acid or propionic acid

#### 5.4   **Instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging**

At the end of the treatment, dispose unused product and the packaging in accordance with local requirements. Used product can be flushed to municipal sewer or disposed to the manure deposit depending on local requirements. Avoid release to an individual waste water treatment plant.

#### 5.5   **Conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage**

Do not store above 30 °C and store protected from sunlight. Keep in original container.

Store in dry, cool, well-ventilated area. Provide local exhaust or general room ventilation to minimize mist and/or vapour concentrations. Keep container closed when not in use. Minimize exposure to air and light.

The shelf-life of the product is 9 months. After opening the cans, products are stable during 6 months.

Sodium chlorate may be formed during storage. To avoid increasing formation of this degradation product, do not exceed the established shelf life of the biocidal product.

### 6 OTHER INFORMATION

In the eventuality of abstraction of drinking water from surface water/groundwater, the chlorate drinking water limit of 700 μg/L should be monitored.

‘Professionals (including industrial users)’ means trained professional if this is required by national legislation.

The full titles of the EN standards referenced in sections ‘general risk mitigation measures’ are :

EN ISO 374-1:2016 Protective gloves against dangerous chemicals and micro-organisms – Part 1: Terminology and performance requirements for chemical risks (ISO 374-1:2016)

EN 166:2001 Personal eye-protection – Specifications

EN 14605:2005+A1:2009 Protective clothing against liquid chemicals – performance requirements for clothing with liquid-tight (Type 3) or spray-tight (Type 4) connections, including items providing protection to parts of the body only (Types PB [3] and PB [4])

EN 13832-3:2018 Footwear protecting against chemicals – Part 3: Requirements for prolonged contact with chemicals

EN 136:1998 Respiratory protective devices – Full face masks – Requirements, testing, marking EN 136:1998/AC:2003

EN 143:2021 Respiratory protective devices – Particle filters – Requirements, testing, marking

Council Directive 98/24/EC of 7 April 1998 on the protection of the health and safety of workers from the risks related to chemical agents at work (fourteenth individual Directive within the meaning of Article 16(1) of Directive 89/391/EEC) ([OJ L 131, 5.5.1998, p. 11](http://publications.europa.eu/resource/oj/JOL_1998_131_R_TOC))

### **PT2 - Use # 6.1 - CIP procedures with or without circulation in industries - professionals**

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Product type | PT02: Disinfectants and algaecides not intended for direct application to humans or animals |
| Where relevant, an exact description of the authorised use | Not relevant |
| Target organism(s) (including development stage) | Scientific name: no data Common name: Bacteria Development stage: . Scientific name: no data Common name: Yeasts Development stage: . Scientific name: no data Common name: Viruses Development stage: . |
| Field(s) of use | indoor use In industry: Disinfection of inner hard/non-porous surfaces by CIP with or without circulation, with or without prior cleaning. |
| Application method(s) | Method: CIP procedures Detailed description: Concentrated product, to be diluted.For disinfection procedure by CIP with or without circulation. Product is added into the system by dosing the recommended dilution (closed system). The pipeline or tank is rinsed with water of drinking water quality under closed system after contact time. |
| Application rate(s) and frequency | Application Rate: . 1) CIP procedures (with circulation) WITH prior cleaning: # at +20 °C in 15 min contact time : Active against bacteria & yeasts : 0,5 % Active against bacteria, yeasts and viruses : 1 % # at +55 °C in 30 sec. contact time : Active against bacteria, yeasts and viruses : 2 % Application Rate: . 2) CIP procedures (with circulation) without prior cleaning : # at +20 °C in 15 min contact time : Active against bacteria, yeasts and viruses : 2,5 % Application Rate: . 3) CIP procedures (without circulation) WITH prior cleaning : # at +20 °C in 15 min contact time : Active against bacteria & yeasts and viruses : 1 % # at +55 °C in 2 min contact time : Active against bacteria, yeasts and viruses : 2 % Application Rate: . 4) Active against P.aeruginosa- & S.aureus-biofilms: at 1 % at +20 °C in 60 min contact time, without prior cleaning Number and timing of application: Frequency: To be used in between production cycles as intermediate cleaning and/or part of daily cleaning |
| Category(ies) of users | professional |
| Pack sizes and packaging material | — **Bottle**: 1 L, 1 Kg — **Drum**: 5 L, 10 L, 20 L, 25 L, 30 L, 60 L, 200 L, 220 L, 5 kg, 10 kg, 12 kg, 20 kg, 25 kg, 30 kg, 60 kg, 200 kg, 220 kg, 240 kg — **Container:** 600 L, 1 000 L, 1 100 L, 600 kg, 1 000 kg, 1 100 kg HDPE (High Density Polyethylene) |

##### 4.1.1   *Use-specific instructions for use*

Mixing and loading:

Manual mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and product is poured in the recipient containing the relevant volume of water to obtain the necessary dilution.

Semi-automated mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and hose is placed in the can. Then transfer the product by manual pumping or with a semi-automatic pump.

Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Dilution:

See application rates & frequency section.

Application:

In clean conditions: Prior to disinfection, clean the surfaces.

Application in CIP with circulation: Add the product and water into the system to obtain the required concentration of use. Circulate for the required contact time at the required temperature.

Application in CIP without circulation: Add the product and water into the system to obtain the required concentration of use. Use enough working solution to cover all surfaces. Inner surfaces should remain wet during the required contact time at the required temperature.

Post-application: The pipeline or tank is rinsed with water of drinking water quality under closed system. Empty containers are disposed of.

Frequency: To be used in between production cycles as intermediate cleaning and/or part of daily cleaning.

##### 4.1.2   *Use-specific risk mitigation measures*

See general directions for use.

### 5 GENERAL DIRECTIONS FOR USE OF THE META SPC 6

#### 5.1   **Instructions for use**

See use-specific instructions for each use

Do not mix with other chemicals.

The surfaces to be disinfected must be cleaned before the disinfection procedure if required.

For the relevant uses, the following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘Thoroughly clean, rinse and remove excess water on the surfaces to be disinfected’.

Disinfection cycle :

- — Products must be diluted in clean water before use.

- — Dilution rate & contact time depends on the use considered. Please refer to the description of application method related to each use.

- — Final rinsing (with water of drinking water quality) is required.

Surface disinfection procedures by soaking/dipping/CIP/closed systems : According to the Applicant, the soaking/dipping/CIP solution is prepared ad hoc (never in advance) for a single-time use. The bath/CIP solution is not intended to be re-used.

The following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘The soaking/dipping bath/CIP solution should be prepared ad hoc (never in advance) and used only once’.

#### 5.2   **Risk mitigation measures**

During mixing and loading step, maintenance and repair:

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves in line with EN 374 or equivalent during product handling phase (glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

The use of eye protection in line with EN 166 or equivalent during handling of the product is mandatory.

A protective coverall (at least type 3, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

Maintenance and repair, pressurized systems:

Use of respiratory protective equipment (RPE) in line with EN 136 or equivalent providing a protection factor of 10 is mandatory. At least a powered air purifying respirator with helmet/hood/mask (TH1/TM1), or a half/full mask with particle filter P2 is required.

The use of these protective equipment is without prejudice to the application of Council Directive 98/24/EC and other Union legislation in the area of health and safety at work. See section 6 for the full references to this act and the European Standards.

Ensure adequate ventilation during the application.

After disinfection, treated surfaces should be thoroughly rinsed with water of drinking water quality.

#### 5.3   **Particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment**

IF INHALED: Move to fresh air and keep at rest in a position comfortable for breathing.

If symptoms: Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

If no symptoms: Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF SWALLOWED: Immediately rinse mouth. Give something to drink, if exposed person is able to swallow. Do NOT induce vomiting. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

IF ON SKIN: Immediately wash skin with plenty of water. Thereafter take off all contaminated clothing and wash it before reuse. Continue to wash the skin with water for 15 minutes. Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF IN EYES: Immediately rinse with water for several minutes. Remove contact lenses, if present and easy to do. Continue rinsing for at least 15 minutes. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

Information to Healthcare personnel/doctor:

The eyes should also be rinsed repeatedly on the way to the doctor if eye exposure to alkaline chemicals (pH > 11), amines and acids like acetic acid, formic acid or propionic acid

#### 5.4   **Instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging**

At the end of the treatment, dispose unused product and the packaging in accordance with local requirements. Used product can be flushed to municipal sewer or disposed to the manure deposit depending on local requirements. Avoid release to an individual waste water treatment plant.

#### 5.5   **Conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage**

Do not store above 30 °C and store protected from sunlight. Keep in original container .

Store in dry, cool, well-ventilated area. Provide local exhaust or general room ventilation to minimize mist and/or vapour concentrations. Keep container closed when not in use. Minimize exposure to air and light.

The shelf-life of the product is 9 months. After opening the cans, products are stable during 6 months.

Sodium chlorate may be formed during storage. To avoid increasing formation of this degradation product, do not exceed the established shelf life of the biocidal product.

### 6 OTHER INFORMATION

In the eventuality of abstraction of drinking water from surface water/groundwater, the chlorate drinking water limit of 700 μg/L should be monitored.

#### 4.1   **Use description**

### **PT2 Use # 7.1 – Disinfection of hard non-porous surfaces and equipment by brushing, foaming, spraying, pouring followed by wiping - professional**

| --- | --- |

Scientific name: no data Common name: fungi Development stage: . Scientific name: no data Common name: Viruses Development stage: . |

| Application method(s) | Method: Surface disinfection by brushing, foaming, spraying, pouring followed by wiping Detailed description: Concentrated product, to be diluted. For disinfection procedure by :- Brushing, - Foaming: low pressure foaming device - Spraying: low pressure coarse spraying device - Pouring followed by wiping. Rinse with water of drinking water quality after disinfection. |
| Application rate(s) and frequency | Application Rate: Maximum application rate: 250 mL/m<sup>2</sup>. Active against bacteria, fungi/yeasts and viruses at Room Temperature in 20 min contact time : 4 % Number and timing of application: Frequency: To be used in between production and/or part of daily cleaning |
| Category(ies) of users | professional |
| Pack sizes and packaging material | — **Bottle**: 1 L, 1 Kg — **Drum**: 5 L, 10 L, 20 L, 25 L, 30 L, 60 L, 200 L, 220 L, 5 kg, 10 kg, 12 kg, 20 kg, 25 kg, 30 kg, 60 kg, 200 kg, 220 kg, 240 kg — **Container:** 600 L, 1 000 L, 1 100 L, 600 kg, 1 000 kg, 1 100 kg HDPE (High Density Polyethylene) |

##### 4.1.1   *Use-specific instructions for use*

Mixing and loading:

Manual mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and product is poured in the recipient containing the relevant volume of water to obtain the necessary dilution.

Semi-automated mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and hose is placed in the can. Then transfer the product by manual pumping or with a semi-automatic pump.

Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Dilution:

Bacteria, yeasts, fungi, virus: Dilute the product at 4 %. (Dose 40 ml product to prepare a 1 L solution)

Application:

Application rate is 250 mL/m<sup>2</sup>. Foaming and spraying are done at low pressure avoiding the formation of aerosols. Make sure to foam/spray/ brush/ pour and wipe sufficient liquid to keep the surface wet for at least 20 minutes.

The application rate is a worst-case value. Apply the minimum quantity of product in order to cover the entire surface and in order for the surface to remain wet until the end of contact time.

Brushing: a brush with a handle has to be used to apply the diluted solution.

Pour the diluted solution directly on the surface and brush.

Rinse the surfaces after use with water of drinking water quality.

Post-application: empty containers are disposed of.

Frequency: To be used in between production and/or part of daily cleaning.

##### 4.1.2   *Use-specific risk mitigation measures*

See general directions for use.

Do not immerse hands in the solution.

Do not (use/apply) directly on or near food, feed or drinks, or on surfaces or utensils likely to be in direct contact with food, feed, drinks and livestock.

Do not apply directly to surfaces on which food or feed is stored, prepared or eaten.

##### 4.1.3   *Where specific to the use, the particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment*

See general directions for use.

##### 4.1.4   *Where specific to the use, the instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging*

See general directions for use.

##### 4.1.5   *Where specific to the use, the conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage*

See general directions for use.

#### 4.2   **Use description**

##### 4.2.1   *Use-specific instructions for use*

Animal houses must be empty of animals during disinfection. The product is used to disinfect animal housings of pigs, cows, poultry and also tanks and equipment. Protect waterers or feeders during disinfection. Clean the surfaces thoroughly with a detergent before disinfection. Rinse with clean water and remove surplus water.

Mixing and loading:

Manual mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and product is poured in the recipient containing the relevant volume of water to obtain the necessary dilution.

Semi-automated mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and hose is placed in the can. Then transfer the product by manual pumping or with a semi-automatic pump.

Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Dilution:

See application rates and frequency section.

- — spraying and foaming:

container is opened manually and emptied into the reservoir or connected to a pump, which pumps the product into the reservoir of the spraying or foaming device which is then filled up with water in order to achieve the required concentration of use.

- — dipping:

containers are opened manually and are emptied into the dipping/immersion baths or connected to a pump, which pumps the product into the dipping/immersion bath to achieve the required concentration of use.

- — brushing and pouring followed by wiping:

add the appropriate amount of the concentrated product manually (or via a connected pump) to water to achieve the required concentration.

Bacteria, yeasts, fungi, virus: Dilute the product at 4 %. (Dose 40 ml product to prepare a 1 L solution)

Application:

- — By spraying, apply the product with a back pulverisator or an automatic sprayer in order to cover the surfaces. Application rate is 150 mL/m<sup>2</sup>. Spraying is done at low pressure avoiding the formation of aerosols. Make sure to spray sufficient liquid to keep the surface wet for at least 30 minutes.

- — By foaming, apply the product with a foaming device in order to cover the surfaces. Application rate is 150 mL/m<sup>2</sup>. Foaming is done at low pressure avoiding the formation of aerosols. Make sure to spray sufficient liquid to keep the surface wet for at least 30 minutes.

- — By dipping, equipment is dipped/immersed into the bath and is removed after 30 minutes.

- — By brushing and pouring followed by wiping: apply the product by pouring sufficient quantity in order to cover the surfaces. Application rate is 150 mL/m<sup>2</sup>. Make sure to pour sufficient liquid to keep the surface wet for at least 30 minutes.

Brushing: a brush with a handle has to be used to apply the diluted solution.

Pour the diluted solution directly on the surface and brush.

The application rate is a worst-case value. Apply the minimum quantity of product in order to cover the entire surface and in order for the surface to remain wet until the end of contact time.

Use only on non-porous surfaces.

Rinse the surfaces after use with water of drinking water quality.

Post-application: Re-entry of animals can be done 24h after the end of the application.

Do not use equipment on animals until it is rinsed and completely dried.

Empty containers are disposed of.

Frequency: To be used in between animal growth cycles.

##### 4.2.2   *Use-specific risk mitigation measures*

See general directions for use.

Do not immerse hands in the solution.

For food commodities, ensure that the concentration of chlorate present in food does not exceed the MRL values set in Regulation 2020/749.

##### 4.2.3   *Where specific to the use, the particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment*

See general directions for use.

See general directions for use.

##### 4.3.1   *Use-specific instructions for use*

Mixing and loading:

Manual mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and product is poured in the recipient containing the relevant volume of water to obtain the necessary dilution.

Semi-automated mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and hose is placed in the can. Then transfer the product by manual pumping or with a semi-automatic pump.

Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Dilution:

Bacteria, yeasts, fungi, virus: Dilute the product at 4 %. (Dose 40 ml product to prepare a 1 L solution)

Application:

The application rate is a worst-case value. Apply the minimum quantity of product in order to cover the entire surface and in order for the surface to remain wet until the end of contact time.

Post-application: Rinse the treated surfaces with water of drinking water quality. Empty containers are disposed of.

Frequency: To be used in between food production and/or part of daily cleaning.

### 5 GENERAL DIRECTIONS FOR USE OF THE META SPC 7

#### 5.1   **Instructions for use**

See use-specific instructions for each use

Do not mix with other chemicals.

The surfaces to be disinfected must be cleaned before the disinfection procedure if required.

For the relevant uses, the following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘Thoroughly clean, rinse and remove excess water on the surfaces to be disinfected’.

Disinfection cycle :

- — Products must be diluted in clean water before use.

- — Dilution rate & contact time depends on the use considered. Please refer to the description of application method related to each use.

- — Final rinsing (with water of drinking water quality) is required.

Surface disinfection procedures by soaking/dipping: According to the Applicant, the soaking/dipping solution is prepared ad hoc (never in advance) for a single-time use. The bath solution is not intended to be re-used.

The following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘The soaking/dipping solution should be prepared ad hoc (never in advance) and used only once’.

Surface disinfection procedures by spraying, foaming, brushing or pouring followed by wiping : The surfaces to be disinfected must be wet enough in order to keep them wet during the preconised contact time for optimal disinfection.

The following precautionary sentence will be added on the product label : ‘Make sure to wet surfaces completely’.

#### 5.2   **Risk mitigation measures**

During mixing & loading, application (brushing/pouring and wiping, dipping) and post application step:

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves during product handling phase in line with EN 374 or equivalent (glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

The use of eye protection in line with EN 166 or equivalent during handling of the product is mandatory.

A protective coverall (at least type 3, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

During application by foaming and by coarse spraying:

Wear protective chemical resistant gloves during product handling phase in line with EN 374 or equivalent (glove material to be specified by the authorisation holder within the product information).

A protective coverall, with hood (at least type 4, EN 14605 or equivalent) shall be worn.

Wearing oversleeves for wrists and ankles is mandatory.

Wear suitable protective footwear (EN 13832 or equivalent) when applying the product.

Using a face shield/face mask is mandatory.

Respiratory protective equipment (RPE) providing a protection factor of 40 is mandatory. At least a powered air purifying respirator with helmet/hood/mask (TH3/TM3), or a full-face mask (EN 136 or equivalent) with particle filter (EN143 filters or equivalent), type P3.

The use of these protective equipment is without prejudice to the application of Council Directive 98/24/EC and other Union legislation in the area of health and safety at work. See section 6 for the full references to this act and the European Standards.

Apply the product with a lance to increase the distance between the user and the source of emission.

After disinfection, treated surfaces should be thoroughly rinsed with water of drinking water quality.

The same PPEs/RPE have to be worn during the rinsing of the room.

Necessary specifications for safe and adequate use of PPE during the application of corrosive product have to be specified by the authorisation holder.

Training must be ensured for the users to ensure appropriate handling of the product and the PPE required.

To limit exposure during the removal of PPEs, PPEs have to be cleaned after each use and before removal.

In order to reduce inhalatory exposure product may only be applied by coarse spraying. Please refer to device supplier for technical details.

Ensure that the application by foaming and by coarse spraying is carried out with an adequate ventilation. The ventilation needs to be specified by the Authorisation holder within the product information.

Wash hands after use.

Apply in the absence of humans and animals and out of their reach.

No access of the general public during treatment and until surfaces are dried.

Keep away from pets/keep out of reach of pets.

#### 5.3   **Particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment**

IF INHALED: Move to fresh air and keep at rest in a position comfortable for breathing.

If symptoms: Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

If no symptoms: Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF SWALLOWED: Immediately rinse mouth. Give something to drink, if exposed person is able to swallow. Do NOT induce vomiting. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

IF ON SKIN: Immediately wash skin with plenty of water. Thereafter take off all contaminated clothing and wash it before reuse. Continue to wash the skin with water for 15 minutes. Call a POISON CENTRE or a doctor.

IF IN EYES: Immediately rinse with water for several minutes. Remove contact lenses, if present and easy to do. Continue rinsing for at least 15 minutes. Call 112/ambulance for medical assistance.

Information to Healthcare personnel/doctor:

The eyes should also be rinsed repeatedly on the way to the doctor if eye exposure to alkaline chemicals (pH > 11), amines and acids like acetic acid, formic acid or propionic acid

#### 5.4   **Instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging**

At the end of the treatment, dispose unused product and the packaging in accordance with local requirements. Used product can be flushed to municipal sewer or disposed to the manure deposit depending on local requirements. Avoid release to an individual waste water treatment plant.

#### 5.5   **Conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage**

Do not store above 30 °C and store protected from sunlight. Keep in original container .

Store in dry, cool, well-ventilated area. Provide local exhaust or general room ventilation to minimize mist and/or vapour concentrations. Keep container closed when not in use. Minimize exposure to air and light.

The shelf-life of the product is 18 months. After opening the cans, products are stable during 6 months.

Sodium chlorate may be formed during storage. To avoid increasing formation of this degradation product, do not exceed the established shelf life of the biocidal product.

### 6 OTHER INFORMATION

In the eventuality of abstraction of drinking water from surface water/groundwater, the chlorate drinking water limit of 700 μg/L should be monitored.

‘Professionals (including industrial users)’ means trained professional if this is required by national legislation.

### 4 AUTHORISED USE(S) OF THE META SPC

#### 4.1   **Use description**

### **PT2 Use # 8.1 – Disinfection of hard non-porous surfaces and equipment by brushing, foaming, spraying, pouring followed by wiping - professional**

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| Product type | PT02: Disinfectants and algaecides not intended for direct application to humans or animals |
| Where relevant, an exact description of the authorised use | Not relevant |

Scientific name: no data Common name: fungi Development stage: . Scientific name: no data Common name: Viruses Development stage: . |
| Field(s) of use | indoor use In Households, industry, institutions (such as offices or prison), public areas and hotels: Disinfection of hard/non-porous surfaces, without prior cleaning |
| Application method(s) | Method: Surface disinfection by brushing, foaming, spraying, pouring followed by wiping Detailed description: Concentrated product, to be diluted. For disinfection procedure by :- Brushing, - Foaming: low pressure foaming device- Spraying: low pressure coarse spraying device- Pouring followed by wiping. Rinse with water of drinking water quality after disinfection. |
| Application rate(s) and frequency | Application Rate: Maximum application rate: 250 mL/m<sup>2</sup>. Active against bacteria, fungi/yeasts and viruses at Room Temperature in 20 min contact time : 4 % Number and timing of application: Frequency: To be used in between production and/or part of daily cleaning |
| Category(ies) of users | professional |
| Pack sizes and packaging material | — **Bottle**: 1 L, 1 Kg — **Drum**: 5 L, 10 L, 20 L, 25 L, 30 L, 60 L, 200 L, 220 L, 5 kg, 10 kg, 12 kg, 20 kg, 25 kg, 30 kg, 60 kg, 200 kg, 220 kg, 240 kg — **Container:** 600 L, 1 000 L, 1 100 L, 600 kg, 1 000 kg, 1 100 kg HDPE (High Density Polyethylene) |

##### 4.1.1   *Use-specific instructions for use*

Mixing and loading:

Manual mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and product is poured in the recipient containing the relevant volume of water to obtain the necessary dilution.

Semi-automated mixing and loading: Container is opened manually and hose is placed in the can. Then transfer the product by manual pumping or with a semi-automatic pump.

Fully automated mixing and loading: Container is opened or connected manually. Transfer of the product is automatically performed.

Dilution:

Bacteria, yeasts, fungi, virus: Dilute the product at 4 %. (Dose 40 ml product to prepare a 1 L solution)

Application:

Application rate is 250 mL/m<sup>2</sup>. Foaming and spraying are done at low pressure avoiding the formation of aerosols. Make sure to foam/spray/ brush/pour and wipe sufficient liquid to keep the surface wet for at least 20 minutes.

Brushing: a brush with a handle has to be used to apply the diluted solution.

Pour the diluted solution directly on the surface and brush.

The application rate is a worst-case value. Apply the minimum quantity of product in order to cover the entire surface and in order for the surface to remain wet until the end of contact time.

Rinse the surfaces after use with water of drinking water quality.

Post-application: empty containers are disposed of.

Frequency: To be used in between production and/or part of daily cleaning.

##### 4.1.2   *Use-specific risk mitigation measures*

See general directions for use.

Do not immerse hands in the solution.

Do not (use/apply) directly on or near food, feed or drinks, or on surfaces or utensils likely to be in direct contact with food, feed, drinks and livestock.

Do not apply directly to surfaces on which food or feed is stored, prepared or eaten.

##### 4.1.3   *Where specific to the use, the particulars of likely direct or indirect effects, first aid instructions and emergency measures to protect the environment*

See general directions for use.

##### 4.1.4   *Where specific to the use, the instructions for safe disposal of the product and its packaging*

See general directions for use.

##### 4.1.5   *Where specific to the use, the conditions of storage and shelf-life of the product under normal conditions of storage*

See general directions for use.

#### 4.2   **Use description**

### **PT3 Use # 8.2 – Disinfection of hard non-porous surfaces and equipment by brushing, foaming, spraying, pouring followed by wiping, dipping - professional**
